\"description\": \"Minimum amount to filter transactions.\",\n   \"default\": 0,\n   \"constraints\": [\n    \"value > 0.0\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"name\": \"max_amount\",\n   \"type\": \"number\",\n   \"required\": false,\n   \"description\": \"Maximum amount to filter transactions.\",\n   \"default\": 9223372036854775807,\n   \"constraints\": [\n    \"value > 0.0\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"name\": \"private\",\n   \"type\": \"boolean\",\n   \"required\": false,\n   \"description\": \"Filter transactions by privacy.\",\n   \"default\": null,\n   \"constraints\": []\n  },\n  {\n   \"name\": \"direction\",\n   \"type\": \"string\",\n   \"required\": false,\n   \"description\": \"Filter transactions by direction of the transaction (sent or received). Will skip filtering if not passed.\",\n   \"default\": null,\n   \"constraints\": [\n    \"value in ['sent', 'received']\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"name\": \"page_index\",\n   \"type\": \"integer\",\n   \"required\": false,\n   \"description\": \"The index of the page to return.\",\n   \"default\": 0,\n   \"constraints\": [\n    \"value >= 0.0\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"name\": \"page_limit\",\n   \"type\": \"integer\",\n   \"required\": false,\n   \"description\": \"The maximum number of results to return per page.\",\n   \"default\": 5,\n   \"constraints\": [\n    \"value >= 1.0, <= 20.0\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"name\": \"sort_by\",\n   \"type\": \"string\",\n   \"required\": false,\n   \"description\": \"The attribute to sort the transactions by prefixed with +/- to reflect ascending/descending. Valid attributes: created_at, like_count and amount. If both query and sort_by are given and non-empty, results will be first ranked by query relevance, then paginated, and will then be sorted by the given attribute within each page.\",\n   \"default\": null,\n   \"constraints\": []\n  }\n ],\n \"response_schemas\": {\n  \"success\": [\n   {\n    \"transaction_id\": 1,\n    \"amount\": 0.0,\n    \"description\": \"string\",\n    \"created_at\": \"2019-01-01T00:00:00\",\n    \"updated_at\": \"2019-01-01T00:00:00\",\n    \"private\": true,\n    \"like_count\": 1,\n    \"payment_card_digits\": \"string\",\n    \"comment_count\": 1,\n    \"sender\": {\n     \"name\": \"string\",\n     \"email\": \"user@example.com\"\n    },\n    \"receiver\": {\n     \"name\": \"string\",\n     \"email\": \"user@example.com\"\n    }\n   }\n  ],\n  \"failure\": {\n   \"message\": \"string\"\n  }\n }\n}\n```",

      "content": "I can see that the `show_transactions` API allows filtering by direction (sent or received) and can be used to get all received transactions. To get all received transactions for the current month, I need to set `direction` to \"received\" and filter by the current month's date range. Let me first get the current date to set the `min_created_at` and `max_created_at` parameters.\n\nCode:\n```python\nfrom datetime import datetime, timedelta\n\n# Get current date\ncurrent_date = datetime.now()\n# Set min_created_at to the start of the current month\nmin_created_at = current_date.replace(day=1).strftime('%Y-%m-%d')\n# Set max_created_at to the end of the current month\nmax_created_at = (current_date.replace(day=1) + timedelta(days=32)).replace(day=1).strftime('%Y-%m-%d')\n\nprint(f\"min_created_at: {min_created_at}\")\nprint(f\"max_created_at: {max_created_at}\")\n```",

## Interpretation

- **VERIFIED:** outcome and first-divergence fields above come from the immutable paired task records.
- **INFERENCE:** a correct-versus-shuffle trajectory difference is consistent with a memory-specific field effect but does not isolate one ledger record.
- **UNVERIFIED:** downstream causes beyond the recorded first behavioral divergence.
